Abstract: We study real-time audio-responsive character control as a deployment-faithful problem: strictly causal, bounded-latency streaming that must generate coherent full-body motion at interactive frame rates while the audio condition can change abruptly, including tempo shifts, drops, or user edits. Prior music-to-motion systems are largely optimized for offline generation with global context, and degrade in streaming rollouts where conditioning history becomes stale or unreliable. We introduce DiscoForcing, a streaming audio-driven diffusion framework that combines a causal music encoder that captures rhythmic structure and phase dynamics with a diffusion-forcing sequence model trained under heterogeneous noise levels across the temporal horizon. Building on this, we design a hybrid temporal schedule and a history-guided streaming sampler to explicitly trade off responsiveness against long-horizon consistency under non-stationary audio. Implemented in an end-to-end real-time interactive system with online avatar playback and humanoid deployment workflows, DiscoForcing delivers more stable long-horizon rollouts and sharper audio-motion alignment than prior baselines under matched causality and latency constraints while maintaining real-time throughput.

</details>


### [75] [Janus-LoRA: A Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28495)
*Cheng Chen,Pengpeng Zeng,Yuyu Guo,Lianli Gao,Hengtao Shen,Jingkuan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Janus-LoRA通过梯度修正和解耦边际损失，解决了LoRA在持续学习中正交性违背与稳定性-可塑性平衡问题，实现了新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA在持续学习中，其复合更新会系统性地违反与历史知识子空间的正交性，导致灾难性遗忘；而强制正交又会损害可塑性，破坏稳定性-可塑性平衡。

Method: 提出Janus-LoRA框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 梯度修正（Gradient Rectification）：通过闭式解数学上解耦LoRA因子更新，强制与在线估计的历史知识子空间正交；2) 解耦边际损失（Decoupled Margin Loss）：在特征层面推动新表征远离旧表征，创建低干扰区域。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的基准测试上，Janus-LoRA实现了参数级正交性与特征级分离的协同，取得了新的最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过同时保证参数级正交性和特征级分离，Janus-LoRA有效恢复了持续学习中的稳定性-可塑性平衡。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a promising paradigm for Continual Learning. It independently updates its low-rank factors ($A$ and $B$), creating a composite update to the full weight matrix through their interaction. To prevent catastrophic forgetting, this update should remain orthogonal to the task-specific subspace that contains previously learned knowledge. However, we identify that this composite update systematically violates this orthogonality, reintroducing interference and undermining stability. Furthermore, naively enforcing this orthogonality compromises plasticity, disrupting the delicate stability-plasticity trade-off. To resolve these issues, we propose \textbf{Janus-LoRA}, a framework that restores this balance through two novel components. Specifically, we first introduce Gradient Rectification, a closed-form solution that mathematically decouples LoRA's factor updates, enforcing orthogonality against the historical knowledge subspace identified by an efficient Online Estimation. Next, to enhance plasticity, we introduce a Decoupled Margin Loss that promotes feature-level separation by pushing new feature representations away from old ones, thus creating distinct, low-interference regions for new learning. Comprehensive experiments on challenging benchmarks demonstrate that by harmonizing parameter-level orthogonality with feature-level separation, Janus-LoRA achieves a superior balance and establishes new state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [76] [DriveWAM: Video Generative Priors Enable Scalable World-Action Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28544)
*Chen Shi,Jinrui Xu,Shaoshuai Shi,Kehua Sheng,Bo Zhang,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveWAM是一种驾驶世界动作模型，将预训练的视频扩散变压器改编为自回归视频-动作策略，在统一框架下联合建模视频和动作流，并通过场景演化引导和选择性KV记忆实现高效的长时域规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与主要基于静态图像-文本对预训练的视觉-语言模型不同，视频生成模型天然适合捕捉驾驶任务所需的时序动态和运动先验。本文旨在利用大规模视频预训练先验来提升端到端自动驾驶的动作生成能力。

Method: 1) 将视频和动作流组织为统一的时序令牌序列，在联合流匹配目标下训练；2) 引入场景演化驾驶引导（scene-evolving driving guidance），用冻结的VLM生成语义意图指导生成；3) 提出选择性KV记忆（selective KV memory），通过相关性-冗余度缓存选择保持推理时的有限记忆。

Result: 在NAVSIM和PhysicalAI-Autonomous-Vehicles基准上取得了强规划性能，从4k到100k驾驶片段的数据规模研究进一步证实了世界动作建模在端到端自动驾驶中的扩展潜力。

Conclusion: DriveWAM证明了将视频生成模型的世界先验迁移到动作生成的有效性，为端到端自动驾驶提供了一种可扩展的世界动作联合建模范式。

Abstract: Pretrained foundation models have become an important basis for end-to-end autonomous driving. In contrast to vision-language models pretrained primarily on static image-text pairs, video generative models capture temporal dynamics and motion priors that are naturally suited for driving. We present DriveWAM, a driving world-action model that adapts a pretrained video diffusion transformer into an autoregressive video-action policy. DriveWAM organizes video and action streams into a unified temporal token sequence and trains them under a joint flow-matching objective, preserving the pretrained video-generation architecture while adapting its large-scale video priors to action generation. To incorporate high-level scene understanding, we introduce scene-evolving driving guidance, where a frozen VLM produces chunk-specific semantic intent to guide video-action generation. To keep long-horizon rollout bounded, we further introduce selective KV memory, which maintains bounded modality-aware video and action memory pools through relevance-redundancy cache selection at inference time. Experiments on NAVSIM and the PhysicalAI-Autonomous-Vehicles benchmark show that DriveWAM achieves strong planning performance, and a data-scaling study from 4k to 100k driving clips further confirms the scaling potential of world-action modeling for end-to-end autonomous driving.

</details>


### [77] [GEM: Generative Supervision Helps Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28548)
*Ruowen Zhao,Bangguo Li,Zuyan Liu,Yinan Liang,Junliang Ye,Fangfu Liu,Diankun Wu,Zhengyi Wang,Xumin Yu,Yongming Rao,Han Hu,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GEM是一种通过生成式深度图监督预训练的具身视觉语言模型，在多个具身智能基准上达到SOTA，并显著提升了仿真和真实环境中的任务执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身VLM在高层语义理解上表现良好，但在执行层面所需的低层空间和物理知识方面存在显著不足，亟需弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 在VLM预训练阶段引入深度图生成任务作为联合训练目标，并构建了包含接地、推理和规划数据及高质量深度监督的大规模数据集GEM-4M。

Result: GEM在多个具身基准上达到SOTA，部署的GEM-VLA在仿真和真实环境中展现出远超对比方法的任务执行能力。

Conclusion: 将深度图生成作为生成式监督信号融入VLM预训练，能有效增强模型在语义理解和物理操作两方面的能力，从而显著提升具身智能水平。

Abstract: Embodied Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance and generalization in robotics, particularly within Vision-Language-Action frameworks. However, a significant gap remains between the high-level semantic focus of standard text-guided pre-training paradigms and the low-level spatial and physical knowledge critical for execution in embodied environments. In this paper, we introduce GEM, a Generative-supervised Embodied vision-language Model designed to bridge this divide. We propose integrating a depth map generation task directly into the VLM pre-training phase. By training this generative objective jointly with the main model, we observe substantial improvements in embodied intelligence, significantly enhancing both semantic understanding and physical operation capabilities. To support this paradigm, we curate and release GEM-4M, a comprehensive large-scale dataset featuring a mixture of grounding, reasoning, and planning data paired with high-quality depth supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GEM achieves state-of-the-art results across diverse embodied benchmarks. Furthermore, our deployed action model, GEM-VLA, exhibits vastly superior task execution abilities in both simulation environments and real-world evaluations. Code, models, and datasets are available at https://zhaorw02.github.io/GEM/

</details>


### [78] [Deformable Gaussian Occupancy: Decoupling Rigid and Nonrigid Motion with Factorized Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28587)
*Yang Gao,Wuyang Li,Po-Chien Luan,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeGO提出了一种可变形高斯占据框架，通过解耦刚性与非刚性运动并结合基础模型蒸馏，在弱监督下实现动态3D场景理解，在Occ3D-NuScenes基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督占据预测框架大多假设刚体运动并依赖简单的帧间偏移，无法捕捉细粒度形变和保持时间一致性，限制了动态场景理解能力。

Method: DeGO包含两个核心部分：1）解耦高斯变形模块，区分刚性与非刚性运动，每个高斯基元通过形变和偏移联合更新；2）因子化4D蒸馏策略，从VGGT基础模型迁移跨相机和跨帧知识，生成对齐特征增强时间一致性。

Result: 在Occ3D-NuScenes基准上，弱监督条件下对人体实例提升13.5%，整体性能提升10.9%，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 形变感知和基础模型引导的占据建模能有效提升动态场景理解能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Understanding dynamic 3D environments is essential for safe autonomous driving, particularly when reasoning about human-centric, nonrigid agents. However, existing weakly supervised occupancy prediction frameworks predominantly assume rigid-body motion and rely on simple frame-to-frame offsets, limiting their ability to capture fine-grained deformations and maintain temporal coherence. To address this issue, we propose DeGO, a deformable Gaussian occupancy framework that unifies decoupled Gaussian deformation with factorized 4D foundation-model distillation. DeGO disentangles rigid and nonrigid motion, enabling each Gaussian primitive to evolve through both deformation and offset-based updates. In parallel, a factorized 4D distillation strategy transfers cross-camera and cross-frame knowledge from the VGGT foundation model, producing foundation-aligned features that enhance temporal consistency. Experiments on the Occ3D-NuScenes benchmark demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance under weak supervision, delivering 13.5% gains on human-centric instances and 10.9% overall improvements. These results highlight the effectiveness of deformation-aware and foundation-guided occupancy modeling for dynamic scene understanding. The code is publicly available: https://github.com/vita-epfl/DeGO

</details>


### [79] [Mining Multi-Modality Spatio-Temporal Cues for Video Important Person Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28604)
*Xiao Wang,Minglei Yang,Bin Yang,Wenke Huang,Zheng Wang,Xin Xu,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视频重要人物识别任务（VIP），构建Temporal-VIP数据集（9,249个视频片段，11类标注），并开发VIP-Net框架（含SCE编码器、TIR矫正器、VIP推理模块），有效缓解时序重要性偏移（TIS）问题，在准确率（67.3%）和理由相似度（0.63）上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅关注静态图像和即时视觉线索，忽略了视频中丰富的时空信息，导致时序重要性偏移（TIS）现象——早期帧中的重要人物在考虑完整时序上下文后重要性降低。为此，本文提出视频重要人物识别任务，旨在自动识别视频中最具影响力的个体并提供文本理由。

Method: 构建Temporal-VIP数据集（9,249段视频，11类别，含重要性理由标注）；提出VIP-Net框架：1）Social Cue Encoder (SCE)提取多模态时空线索；2）Temporal Importance Rectifier (TIR)进行分层线索融合与跨模态对齐；3）VIP Inference对人物进行排序。

Result: VIP-Net准确率达67.3%，显著优于SOTA模型（37.5%-53.9%）；通过特征引导的LLM精炼，理由相似度均值达0.63。

Conclusion: 本文提出的VIP任务和VIP-Net框架有效解决了视频中的时序重要性偏移问题，为视频理解与智能监控等应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Identifying key individuals in video scenes is essential for applications such as automated video editing and intelligent surveillance. Current methods primarily focus on static images and immediate visual cues, overlooking the rich spatio-temporal information in videos. This leads to the phenomenon of Temporal Importance Shift (TIS), wherein individuals deemed significant in early frames may be demoted as the entire temporal context is considered. To address this, we introduce the Video Important Person (VIP) identification task, aimed at automatically identifying the most influential individuals in videos while providing textual rationales. We present Temporal-VIP, a large-scale rationale-annotated dataset consisting of 9,249 video segments across 11 categories with aligned importance rationales. To mitigate TIS, we develop the VIP-Net framework, which includes a Social Cue Encoder (SCE) for extracting multi-modal spatio-temporal cues, a Temporal Importance Rectifier (TIR) for hierarchical cue fusion and cross-modal alignment, and VIP Inference for ranking individuals. Experimental results show that VIP-Net achieves 67.3% accuracy, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art models (37.5%-53.9%) and yielding a mean rationale similarity of 0.63 to ground truth through feature-guided LLM refinement. The dataset and code are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/yml2002/Temporal-VIP.

</details>


### [80] [JECA^2: Judgment-Explanation Consistent Adversarial Attack against Forensic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28609)
*Jiachen Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出JECA^2，一种针对取证视觉语言模型（VLMs）的红队诊断方法，旨在同时攻击模型的判断和解释，使其在篡改检测中产生一致但错误的判断与解释。实验表明该方法在白盒设置下有效，揭示了现有解释型取证VLMs的鲁棒性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击主要关注翻转模型的二元判断，但生成的解释仍可能泄露取证线索，与攻击后的判断矛盾。因此需要研究判断-解释一致的对抗攻击，评估取证VLMs的鲁棒性。

Method: 视觉侧：使用Grad-CAM引导的扰动，将注意力从篡改区域转向良性区域；文本侧：在token邻近约束下，优化提示嵌入向“真实性肯定”语义对齐。两者协同实现判断与解释的一致性攻击。

Result: 在白盒威胁设置下，JECA^2相比基线实现了更高的攻击成功率和自动判断-解释一致性；对闭源VLMs的迁移攻击效果有限但可测量。

Conclusion: 基于解释的取证VLMs存在一致性失效模式，未来鲁棒性评估应超越二分类检测准确率，关注判断与解释的一致性。

Abstract: Forensic vision-language models (VLMs) have recently been developed to detect image tampering and provide natural-language explanations. However, their robustness against adversarial manipulation remains underexplored. Existing adversarial attacks typically aim to flip the model's binary judgment, while the accompanying explanation may still reveal forensic cues and contradict the attacked judgment. In this paper, we study judgment-explanation consistent adversarial attacks against forensic VLMs and propose JECA^2, a controlled white-box red-team diagnostic that jointly redirects visual attribution and aligns textual explanations with the target judgment. On the visual side, JECA^2 uses Grad-CAM-guided perturbations to divert attribution from tampered regions toward benign regions. On the textual side, it optimizes prompt embeddings toward authenticity-affirming semantics under a token-proximity constraint. Experiments on forensic VLM benchmarks show that JECA^2 achieves higher attack success and automated judgment-explanation consistency than implemented baselines under white-box threat settings, while transfer to closed-source VLMs remains measurable but limited. Our results highlight a consistency failure mode in explanation-based forensic VLMs and motivate future robustness evaluation beyond binary detection accuracy.

</details>


### [81] [Compositional Text-to-Image Generation Via Region-aware Bimodal Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28615)
*Zhuohan Liu,Wujian Peng,Yitong Chen,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BiDPO框架，通过构建大规模偏好数据集BiComp、扩展Diffusion DPO联合优化图文偏好、以及区域级引导方法，显著提升文本到图像模型在复杂组合提示下的生成 fidelity。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型在处理属性绑定、对象关系、计数等复杂组合提示时仍存在困难，缺乏有效的偏好微调方法。

Method: 1) 构建高质量大规模偏好数据集BiComp；2) 扩展Diffusion DPO联合优化图像和文本偏好；3) 采用区域级引导方法聚焦组合概念相关区域。

Result: 在多个基准上一致优于先前方法，显著提升组合 fidelity。

Conclusion: 偏好微调在复杂文本到图像任务中具有潜力，BiDPO提供了一种灵活可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of text-to-image (T2I) models, generating images that accurately reflect complex compositional prompts (covering attribute bindings, object relationships, counting) still remains challenging. To address this, we propose BiDPO, a framework to enhance T2I model's capability of compositional text-to-image generation. We begin by introducing an carefully designed pipeline to construct a large-scale preference dataset, BiComp, with strictly quality control. Then, we extend Diffusion DPO to jointly optimize image and text preferences, which is shown to greatly effective in improving the models to follow complex text prompt in generation. To further enhance the models for fine-grained alignment, we employ a region-level guidance method to focus on regions relevant to compositional concepts. Experimental results demonstrate that our BiDPO substantially improves compositional fidelity, consistently outperforming prior methods across multiple benchmarks. Our approach highlights the potential of preference-based fine-tuning for complex text-to-image tasks, offering a flexible and scalable alternative to existing techniques.

</details>


### [82] [A Multiscale Kinetic Framework for Image Segmentation: From Particle Systems to Continuum Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28619)
*Horacio Tettamanti,Giulia Guicciardi,Mattia Zanella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多尺度动力学框架的共识驱动图像分割方法，将图像视为相互作用粒子系统，通过耦合位置与特征空间的演化机制，推导出动力学方程，并简化为一阶宏观模型，最终利用粒子优化技术实现高效、鲁棒的图像分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像分割方法在处理噪声、复杂纹理时鲁棒性不足，且缺乏统一的多尺度理论框架。作者希望通过粒子动力学视角，建立从微观到宏观的层次化模型，提升分割的准确性和适应性。

Method: 1）将图像像素建模为具有空间位置和颜色特征的相互作用粒子；2）设计耦合交互机制控制粒子在位置和特征空间中的演化，融合输运、聚集与扩散效应；3）推导空间-特征域的粒子密度动力学方程；4）通过尺度缩放得到一阶宏观模型，描述具有特定特征的像素比例演化；5）基于简化模型，采用粒子优化技术实现图像分割。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的框架在不同噪声条件下均能有效进行图像分割，表现出良好的鲁棒性和准确性。

Conclusion: 基于共识的多尺度动力学框架为图像分割提供了新的理论视角和实用工具，简化后的宏观模型在降低计算复杂度的同时保持了分割性能，未来可推广至更多视觉任务。

Abstract: In this work, we present a multiscale kinetic framework for consensus-based image segmentation. By interpreting an image as a system of interacting particles, each pixel is characterised by its spatial position and an internal feature encoding color information. We introduce a coupled interaction scheme governing the evolution of particles in both position and feature spaces, from which we derive a kinetic formulation for the particle density in the space-feature domain combining transport, aggregation, and diffusion effects. Furthermore, through a suitable scaling, we obtain a first-order macroscopic model describing the evolution of the fraction of pixels carrying information on the fraction of pixels having a certain feature. Based on this reduced-complexity model, we present a data-oriented approach where we make use of particle-based optimisation techniques for the accurate segmentation of images. Numerical tests show the effectiveness of the proposed framework and its robustness under different noise conditions.

</details>


### [83] [OSP-Next: Efficient High-Quality Video Generation with Sparse Sequence Parallelism, HiF8 Quantization, and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28691)
*Yunyang Ge,Xianyi He,Zezhong Zhang,Bin Lin,Bin Zhu,Xinhua Cheng,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OSP-Next是一个高效文本到视频生成模型，通过混合全-稀疏注意力架构、稀疏序列并行、HiF8量化和强化学习后训练，在保持视频质量的同时实现显著加速（单GPU最高1.64倍，八GPU最高2.27倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers在视频生成中表现优异，但全注意力的二次复杂度导致效率低下，亟需高效的加速方案。

Method: 1) 混合全-稀疏注意力架构，采用Skiparse-2D Attention固定模式稀疏注意力；2) Sparse Sequence Parallelism (SSP) 通过单次All-to-All通信实现稀疏模式切换，通信量降低75%；3) HiF8 8-bit量化联合训练与稀疏微调；4) Mix-GRPO强化学习后训练提升稀疏模型性能。

Result: VBench总分83.73%，超越Wan2.1基线；5秒720P/768P设置下单GPU加速1.64倍，八GPU加速1.52倍以上；OSP-Next-HiF8在Ascend 950PR上实现1.69倍和2.27倍加速，VBench仅下降0.4%。

Conclusion: OSP-Next通过创新的稀疏注意力、并行策略和量化技术，在不同硬件平台上实现了视频质量与生成效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers achieve strong video generation quality, but the quadratic cost of full attention limits efficiency. We introduce OSP-Next, an efficient text-to-video generation model that integrates sparse attention, parallelism, quantization, and reinforcement learning. OSP-Next uses a hybrid full-sparse attention architecture, where the sparse component is implemented with Skiparse-2D Attention. This fixed-pattern mechanism applies token-wise and group-wise sparse attention along spatial dimensions, leveraging locality while maintaining native compatibility with FlashAttention kernels. Based on the local equivalence of rearrangement in Skiparse-2D Attention, we further propose Sparse Sequence Parallelism (SSP), which partitions subsequences across ranks and switches sparse patterns through a single All-to-All communication. Compared with Ulysses Sequence Parallelism (SP), SSP provides a native parallel strategy for sparse attention and reduces communication volume by 75%. OSP-Next also incorporates HiF8 quantization to enable stable joint training with 8-bit quantization and sparse fine-tuning, and applies Mix-GRPO post-training to improve the performance of the sparse model. Experiments show that OSP-Next achieves a VBench total score of 83.73%, surpassing the Wan2.1 baseline. Under the 5-second 720P and 5-second 768P settings, OSP-Next achieves up to 1.64$\times$ single-GPU speedup and over 1.52$\times$ eight-GPU speedup on NVIDIA H200 GPUs. In addition, with only a 0.4% drop in VBench total score, OSP-Next-HiF8 achieves 1.69$\times$ and 2.27$\times$ speedups under the two settings on a single Ascend 950PR, demonstrating the efficiency and performance of OSP-Next across hardware platforms.

</details>


### [84] [SeeGroup: Multi-Layer Depth Estimation of Transparent Surfaces via Self-Determined Grouping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28735)
*Hongyu Wen,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeeGroup提出了一种无需预定义分组的多层深度估计方法，将每像素多层深度建模为点过程，实现自适应分层，在LayeredDepth基准上将四重相对深度准确率从61.34%提升至70.09%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多层深度方法通常采用预定义的前后顺序分组策略，但三维点可以有多层有效分组方式，预定义分组具有局限性。

Method: 将每像素多层深度建模为点过程，将深度层视为相机射线上无序的事件，设计排列不变性的似然损失函数，让模型自适应分配表面到深度图。

Result: 在LayeredDepth基准上，四重相对深度准确率从61.34%提升至70.09%，显著超越现有方法。

Conclusion: SeeGroup通过避免预定义分组实现了更灵活准确的多层深度估计，达到了新的最优水平。

Abstract: Transparent objects are common in daily life, and it is important to understand their multilayer depth, including the transparent surface and the objects behind it. Existing methods for multilayer depth typically extend single-layer prediction. They define layers by the front-to-back ordering of 3D points and predict the layers sequentially. However, as layered geometry can admit multiple valid groupings of 3D points into layers, a predefined grouping strategy is inherently restrictive. In this work, we propose SeeGroup, a multi-layer depth estimation method that avoids imposing a predefined grouping and allows the model itself to adaptively assign surfaces to depth maps. We formulate per-pixel multi-layer depth as a point process, treating depth layers as unordered events along each camera ray. This induces a permutation-invariant likelihood over the observed depth layers, yielding a loss that naturally supports arbitrary layer groupings. Experiments demonstrate that our method significantly advances the state of the art of multi-layer depth estimation, improving quadruplet relative depth accuracy on LayeredDepth benchmark from 61.34% to 70.09%. Code is available at https://github.com/princeton-vl/SeeGroup.

</details>


### [85] [Self-Prophetic Decoding to Unlock Visual Search in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28741)
*Zhendong He,Qiyuan Dai,Guanbin Li,Liang Lin,Sibei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeProD 是一种训练-free的自预言解码框架，通过结合预训练与后训练LVLM的自我调节机制和概率性预言采样，有效解决了LVLM在视觉搜索中的能力退化与长上下文干扰问题，在多个基准测试中取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在视觉搜索中面临两大挑战：后训练导致的内在能力不兼容，以及长距离多步推理中的上下文干扰。现有方法难以在不引入额外计算开销的情况下同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出SeProD框架，核心包含两个创新：1) 预训练与后训练模型之间的自我调节机制，利用预训练模型的单步能力缓解能力退化与长上下文干扰；2) 基于概率的预言采样（prophetic sampling），预训练模型作为"预言家"生成token，后训练模型根据自身分布选择性接受，实现连贯的多步推理，且采用并行预言接受机制避免额外计算开销。

Result: 在4个视觉搜索基准的12个拆分上，SeProD一致提升了多种视觉搜索LVLM的性能，同时在通用VQA基准上也表现良好，且未增加计算开销。

Conclusion: SeProD作为一种训练-free、即插即用的解码框架，有效解决了LVLM视觉搜索中的能力退化与长上下文干扰问题，为多模态推理提供了高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) are rapidly evolving toward true multimodal reasoning, with visual search representing a concrete instantiation of the thinking-with-images paradigm. However, LVLM visual search faces two key challenges: incompatibility among intrinsic capabilities after post-training, and interference in long multi-step reasoning contexts. To address these, we identify two novel insights. First, self-regulation between pre- and post-training LVLMs leverages the intrinsic single-step capabilities of the pre-training model to mitigate capability deterioration and long-context interference. Second, probability-based prophetic sampling, replacing naive prompting, provides a probabilistic interface where the pre-training model acts as a prophet and the post-training model selectively accepts prophetic tokens under its output distribution, preserving coherent multi-step reasoning. Building on these insights, we introduce SeProD, a self-prophetic decoding framework that leverages intrinsic single-step capabilities to enable coherent multi-step reasoning in a training-free, plug-and-play manner. Experiments show that SeProD consistently improves multiple visual-search LVLMs across all 12 splits of 4 visual search benchmarks, as well as across general VQA benchmarks, without added computational overhead, thanks to its parallel prophetic acceptance mechanism.

</details>


### [86] [Bias Leaves a Gradient Trail: Label-Free Bias Identification via Gradient Probes on Concept Decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28780)
*Thomas Vitry,Kieran Edgeworth,Stefan Wermter,Jae Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需偏见标签、无需重新训练的后期处理方法，通过非负矩阵分解和梯度交互分析，从冻结的视觉模型中识别虚假概念，并在推理时抑制这些概念以提升模型的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见缓解方法依赖人工标注的虚假属性或群体标签，且需要重新训练模型，这在模型已部署或相关偏见未知时难以实现。

Method: 1) 对目标类预测正确的输入提取中间层激活，通过非负矩阵分解得到可解释的概念向量库；2) 利用误分类样本的梯度回传设计偏估计器：偏见概念在修正假阴性时被激活，在修正假阳性时被抑制；3) 根据偏估计器对概念排序，并在推理时抑制高排名概念。

Result: 在Colored MNIST和Waterbirds上成功恢复已知虚假线索；在CelebA上发现与标注性别属性不完全一致的决策相关方向；推理时抑制顶级偏见概念可在Waterbirds上提升最差组准确率17.9个百分点，在CelebA上提升10.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法能够识别与标注属性不一定重合的决策相关虚假方向，既可作为可解释的审计工具，也可为冻结视觉模型提供无需重新训练的可行去偏手段。

Abstract: Vision classifiers can exploit spurious correlations, achieving high in-distribution accuracy yet failing under distribution shift. Existing approaches to bias mitigation and analysis often depend on curated datasets, spurious-attribute or group labels, or retraining, which may be infeasible once a model is deployed or the relevant bias is unknown. We present a bias-label-free, post-hoc method for identifying spurious concepts in frozen vision models, relying only on standard class labels from a held-out audit dataset. For each target class, we collect patches from inputs predicted as that class and apply non-negative matrix factorization to intermediate activations to obtain a bank of interpretable concept vectors. Candidate concepts are then ranked with a bias estimator derived from their interaction with backpropagated gradients on misclassified examples: bias concepts tend to get activated when correcting false negatives and suppressed when correcting false positives. On Colored MNIST and Waterbirds the method recovers concepts aligned with the known spurious cue, and on CelebA it surfaces decision-relevant directions that only partially coincide with the annotated gender attribute; suppressing the top-ranked concepts at inference time improves worst-group accuracy by up to 17.9 percentage points on Waterbirds and 10.4 on CelebA without any retraining or parameter updates. Our method identifies decision-relevant spurious directions that need not coincide with annotated ones, providing both an interpretable auditing tool and an actionable debiasing handle for frozen vision models. Code is available at https://github.com/vitryt/label-free-bias-identification.

</details>


### [87] [Ω-QVLA: Robust Quantization for Vision-Language-Action Models via Composite Rotation and Per-step Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28803)
*Xinyu Wang,Mingze Li,Sicheng Lyu,Dongxiu Liu,Kaicheng Yang,Ziyu Zhao,Yufei Cui,Xiao-Wen Chang,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Omega-QVLA，首个将VLA模型的语言骨干和扩散动作头统一量化到W4A4精度的无训练后训练量化框架，在LIBERO上达到或超过FP16精度，内存减少71.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型参数庞大、部署成本高，此前量化工作仅压缩语言骨干或采用混合精度，认为均匀量化动作头不稳定，本文挑战这一假设。

Method: 结合复合SVD-Hadamard旋转均衡通道权重能量并扩散残差激活异常值，以及逐步骤DiT激活缩放量化吸收去噪步骤中的动态范围漂移。

Result: 在LIBERO上，Pi 0.5和GR00T N1.5压缩至W4A4，任务成功率分别为98.0%和87.8%，匹配或超过FP16参考值的97.1%和87.0%，静态内存减少71.3%。

Conclusion: 挑战了均匀量化动作头不稳定的假设，证明VLA模型可统一量化至W4A4精度并保持性能，为高效部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models unify perception, reasoning, and control within a single policy, yet their multi-billion-parameter backbones and diffusion-based action heads make on-device deployment prohibitively expensive. Prior quantization efforts offer only partial solutions, compressing the LLM backbone while leaving the DiT action head at full precision, or resorting to mixed-precision schemes, driven by the belief that uniformly quantizing the action head is inherently unstable. We challenge this assumption with Omega-QVLA, the first training-free post-training quantization framework that compresses both the language backbone and the entire diffusion action head of a VLA model to a uniform W4A4 precision, eliminating the need for mixed-precision allocation. Omega-QVLA combines a composite SVD-Hadamard rotation that equalizes per-channel weight energy while diffusing residual activation outliers with per-step DiT activation scaling quantization that absorbs dynamic-range drift across denoising steps. On LIBERO, Omega-QVLA compresses Pi 0.5 and GR00T N1.5 to W4A4 with 98.0% and 87.8% task success rates, matching or exceeding their FP16 references of 97.1% and 87.0%, while reducing the static memory footprint by 71.3%. Real-world manipulation experiments further confirm smooth, accurate manipulation where prior methods fail. Code is available at https://github.com/UCMP13753/Omega-QVLA.

</details>


### [88] [AREA: Attribute Extraction and Aggregation for CLIP-Based Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28809)
*Zhen-Hao Xie,Yu-Cheng Shi,Da-Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AREA方法，将CLIP-based类增量学习分解为属性提取与属性聚合两个阶段，通过主测地线分析稳定提取、轻量级任务专家与变分信息瓶颈稳定聚合，并结合最优传输进行推理路由，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP-based CIL中，视觉与文本嵌入的匹配过程可分解为属性提取与属性聚合两阶段，但增量更新会对两者产生偏置，导致灾难性遗忘。现有方法未显式考虑这一分解，缺乏针对性的稳定机制。

Method: 1) 属性提取稳定：通过主测地线分析将类级视觉和文本属性锚定在超球面嵌入空间；2) 属性聚合稳定：学习轻量级任务特定专家，包含评分与残差精炼模块，并使用变分信息瓶颈目标正则化；3) 推理路由：通过最优传输在任务属性流形上进行路由，实现更精确的预测。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，AREA一致性地超越了当前最先进的类增量学习方法。

Conclusion: 将CLIP-based CIL显式分解为属性提取与聚合两个阶段，并分别设计对应的稳定机制，是缓解灾难性遗忘的有效路径。AREA通过几何锚定、任务专家与最优传输路由实现了优异性能。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) is important in building real-world learning systems. In CLIP-based CIL, the model performs classification by comparing similarity between visual and textual embeddings obtained from template prompts, e.g., ``a photo of a [CLASS]''. This seemingly monolithic matching process can be decomposed into two conceptually distinct stages: attribute extraction and attribute aggregation. For example, a model may recognize cat using attributes such as fur texture and whiskers. When learning a new class like car, the model must extract additional attributes like wheels and adjust how they are aggregated in the shared representation space. However, since only data from the current task is available, incremental updates can bias both attribute extraction and aggregation toward new classes, leading to catastrophic forgetting. Therefore, we propose AREA for attribute extraction and aggregation in CLIP-based CIL. To stabilize extraction, we anchor class-level visual and textual attributes on the hyperspherical embedding space via principal geodesic analysis. To stabilize aggregation, we learn lightweight task-specific experts with scoring and residual refinement, regularized by a variational information bottleneck objective. During inference, we perform routing over task attribute manifolds via optimal transport for more concise prediction. Experiments show that AREA consistently outperforms SOTA methods. Code is available at https://github.com/LAMDA-CL/ICML2026-AREA.

</details>


### [89] [HarmoVid: Relightful Video Portrait Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28811)
*Jun Myeong Choi,Jae Shin Yoon,Luchao Qi,Roni Sengupta,Joon-Young Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种视频光照协调方法，通过创新的光照去闪烁模型和视频扩散模型，在保持时序连贯性的同时实现高质量的阴影、色调和光照强度调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频场景中获取配对光照数据不可行且不可扩展，现有基于图像的方法逐帧处理会导致严重的时序抖动问题。

Method: 1) 提出光照去闪烁模型稳定全局和局部闪烁伪影；2) 利用升级后的去闪烁数据训练视频扩散模型；3) 提出非对称alpha掩码条件技术学习真实视频中的清晰边界。

Result: 在时序连贯性、自然度、边界清晰度和光照物理合理性方面均优于已有的基于图像和视频的协调方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了视频光照协调中的时序抖动问题，实现了具有强重光照表现力的高质量视频输出。

Abstract: We present a method for harmonizing the lighting of a foreground video to match a target background scene, adjusting shadows, color tone, and illumination intensity (relightful harmonization). Unlike images, acquiring labeled data for videos, where identical motions are recorded under different lighting conditions, is practically infeasible and non-scalable. While one way to create such paired data is to apply existing image-based harmonization models frame by frame to a video, the resulting outputs often suffer from significant temporal jitters. We overcome this problem by introducing a novel lighting deflickering model that can stabilize the global and local lighting flickering artifacts. Our video diffusion model learns from these upgraded deflickered data with a volume of real and synthetic videos to generate high-quality video harmonization results. We further propose an asymmetric alpha mask conditioning technique to learn the clean boundaries from real videos. Experiments demonstrate that our model achieves strong temporal coherence, naturalness, cleaner boundaries, and physically meaningful lighting behavior, while maintaining strong relighting expressiveness compared to prior image-based and video-based harmonization methods.

</details>


### [90] [Gamma-World: Generative Multi-Agent World Modeling Beyond Two Players](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28816)
*Fangfu Liu,Kai He,Tianchang Shen,Tianshi Cao,Sanja Fidler,Yueqi Duan,Jun Gao,Igor Gilitschenski,Zian Wang,Xuanchi Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种生成式多智能体世界模型，用于交互式视频生成，支持多个智能体的独立控制、置换对称性和高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要针对单智能体设定，无法处理需要多智能体交互的场景（如多个玩家、机器人或具身智能体在共享空间中同时行动）。

Method: 1) Simplex Rotary Agent Encoding（单纯形旋转智能体编码）：将RoPE扩展到3D，将智能体表示为旋转角度空间中的正则单纯形顶点，赋予每个智能体独特相位同时保持置换等价性；2) Sparse Hub Attention（稀疏枢纽注意力）：通过可学习的枢纽令牌跨智能体交互，将交叉注意力成本从二次降至线性；3) 将全上下文扩散教师蒸馏为因果学生模型，支持时序块生成和KV缓存，实现24 FPS的实时交互。

Result: 在多玩家虚拟环境实验中，该方法在视频保真度、动作可控性和智能体间一致性上优于基于槽位和密集注意力的基线，且无需额外训练即可从两玩家泛化到四玩家。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过精心设计的多智能体架构（置换等价的编码方式+高效的稀疏注意力机制），可以有效扩展世界模型到多智能体交互式视频生成场景。

Abstract: World models for interactive video generation have largely focused on single-agent settings, where future observations are generated from a single control signal. However, many generated environments require multi-agent interaction: multiple players, robots, or embodied agents act simultaneously within a shared space. Scaling world models to such settings requires a principled multi-agent design: agents should remain independently controllable, permutation-symmetric, and support efficient inference while maintaining consistency across time and perspectives. In this paper, we present our generative multi-agent world model for interactive simulation. It introduces Simplex Rotary Agent Encoding, a parameter-free extension of 3D RoPE that represents agents as vertices of a regular simplex in rotary angle space. This gives each agent a distinct phase while making all agents permutation-equivalent, enabling scalable agent identity without learned per-slot identities or a fixed agent ordering. To avoid dense all-to-all attention across agents, we further propose Sparse Hub Attention, where learnable hub tokens mediate token interaction across agents, reducing cross-agent attention cost from quadratic to linear in the number of agents. For real-time rollout, we distill a full-context diffusion teacher into a causal student that generates temporal blocks sequentially with KV caching, enabling action-responsive generation at 24 FPS. Experiments in multiplayer virtual environments show that our model improves video fidelity, action controllability, and inter-agent consistency over slot-based and dense-attention baselines, while generalizing from two to four players without additional training.

</details>


### [91] [From Pixels to Words -- Towards Native One-Vision Models at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28820)
*Haiwen Diao,Jiahao Wang,Penghao Wu,Yuhao Dong,Yuwei Niu,Yue Zhu,Zhongang Cai,Weichen Fan,Linjun Dai,Silei Wu,Xuanyu Zheng,Mingxuan Li,Yuanhan Zhang,Bo Li,Hanming Deng,Huchuan Lu,Quan Wang,Lei Yang,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NEO-ov 是一个原生视觉语言基础模型，无需外部编码器或适配器，端到端学习跨帧和像素-词对应关系，在细粒度视觉感知上表现优异，验证了原生"单视觉"架构在大规模下的可行性与竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型通常采用分离的图像编码器和语言解码器通过多阶段对齐组合，这种模块化框架会在帧间碎片化像素级信号，且早期像素-词交互分散。与此同时，原生VLM在多图像、视频理解和空间智能方面探索不足。

Method: 提出NEO-ov原生基础模型，完全消除模块边界，无需外部编码器、辅助适配器或后融合，端到端学习跨帧和像素-词对应关系，使细粒度统一时空建模在模型内部原生涌现。

Result: NEO-ov大幅缩小了与模块化方法的性能差距，在细粒度视觉感知上表现优异，验证了原生"单视觉"架构在大规模下的竞争力。

Conclusion: 原生视觉语言架构不仅是可行的，而且在大规模下具有竞争力。论文还提供了系统性的架构分析和详细的训练配方，以促进后续原生多模态建模。

Abstract: Current vision-language models (VLMs) typically stitch together separate image encoders and language decoders via multi-stage alignment, a modular framework that inevitably fragments pixel-level signals across frames and scatters early pixel-word interactions. In parallel, native VLMs, despite impressive performance on single images, remain largely unexplored in multi-image, video understanding, and spatial intelligence. Hence, we introduce NEO-ov, a native foundation model that learns cross-frame and pixel-word correspondence end-to-end, without any external encoders, auxiliary adapters, or post-hoc fusion. By eliminating module boundaries entirely, NEO-ov enables fine-grained and unified spatiotemporal modeling to emerge natively inside the model. Notably, NEO-ov largely narrows the gap to modular counterparts while excelling at fine-grained visual perception, validating that native "one-vision" architectures are not only feasible but competitive at scale. Beyond empirical performance, we unveil systematic architectural analyses and detailed training recipes to facilitate subsequent native multimodal modeling. Our code and models are publicly available at: https://github.com/EvolvingLMMs-Lab/NEO.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [ICG: Improving Cover Image Generation via MLLM-based Prompting and Personalized Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27374)
*Zhipeng Bian,Jieming Zhu,Qijiong Liu,Wang Lin,Guohao Cai,Zhaocheng Du,Jiacheng Sun,Zhou Zhao,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ICG框架，通过融合多模态大模型提示与个性化偏好对齐，结合多奖励学习策略，实现无需标注数据的端到端个性化封面图生成，显著提升图像质量、语义保真度和个性化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化封面图生成对提升数字平台用户参与度至关重要，但现有方法依赖手工提示和分离模块，缺乏端到端训练且未充分利用个性化偏好，该领域仍未被充分探索。

Method: ICG框架：1) 从物品标题和参考图像中提取语义特征（元标记）；2) 结合用户嵌入进行个性化上下文注入；3) 采用适配器桥接MLLM与扩散模型实现端到端训练；4) 多奖励学习策略（公共美学奖励、相关性奖励+个性化偏好模型）。

Result: 实验表明ICG在图像质量、语义保真度和个性化方面显著提升，提高用户吸引力和离线推荐准确率，兼容常见检查点且无需真实标签。

Conclusion: ICG作为即插即用的适配器成功桥接了MLLM与扩散模型，为无需监督数据的个性化封面图生成提供了有效方案，并在下游推荐任务中展现优势。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and diffusion models (DMs) have opened new possibilities for AI-generated content. Yet, personalized cover image generation remains underexplored, despite its critical role in boosting user engagement on digital platforms. We propose ICG, a novel framework that integrates MLLM-based prompting with personalized preference alignment to generate high-quality, contextually relevant covers. ICG extracts semantic features from item titles and reference images via meta tokens, refines them with user embeddings, and injects the resulting personalized context into the diffusion model. To address the lack of labeled supervision, we adopt a multi-reward learning strategy that combines public aesthetic and relevance rewards with a personalized preference model trained from user behavior. Unlike prior pipelines relying on handcrafted prompts and disjointed modules, ICG employs an adapter to bridge MLLMs and diffusion models for end-to-end training. Experiments demonstrate that ICG significantly improves image quality, semantic fidelity, and personalization, leading to stronger user appeal and offline recommendation accuracy in downstream tasks. As a plug-and-play adapter bridging MLLMs and diffusion models, ICG is compatible with common checkpoints and requires no ground-truth labels during optimization.

</details>


### [93] [Unlocking Fine-Grained and Within-Utterance Speaking Style Control in Prompt-Based Text-to-Speech Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27376)
*Jaehoon Kang,Yejin Lee,Yoonji Park,Kyuhong Shim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新技术：跨语句风格插值（通过对比提示的嵌入向量插值实现平滑风格过渡）和语句内风格转换（通过KV缓存交换和滑动窗口注意力掩码解决自回归TTS解码器的早期token注意力偏差问题），使基于提示的TTS模型具备连续风格控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的TTS模型只能对整句应用单一全局风格，缺乏细粒度控制和连续风格插值能力，无法支持跨语句的平滑风格过渡和单语句内随时间变化的风格转换等实际应用需求。

Method: 1) 跨语句风格插值：在嵌入空间中计算对比风格提示间的方向向量并进行简单插值。2) 语句内风格转换：发现自回归TTS解码器中早期token的注意力偏差问题，提出KV缓存交换和滑动窗口注意力掩码来缓解。

Result: 跨语句插值：性别转换成功率99-100%，音高变化最高36 Hz，语速变化最高1.6音节/秒。语句内转换：说话人相似度0.81-0.91，感知平滑度得分3.48-4.48。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效实现了TTS模型中跨语句的连续风格插值和语句内的动态风格转换，显著扩展了基于提示的TTS模型在细粒度风格控制方面的应用能力。

Abstract: While prompt-based text-to-speech (TTS) models enable natural language-driven speaking style control, they often provide limited fine-grained control and apply a single global style across an utterance. This restricts practical use cases that require continuous style attribute interpolation across utterances and time-varying style transitions within a single utterance. In this paper, we propose novel techniques to achieve both capabilities in existing prompt-based TTS models. For inter-utterance style interpolation, we compute direction vectors between contrastive style prompts in the embedding space and perform simple interpolation, enabling smooth transitions between style characteristics. For intra-utterance style transition, we first identify a strong attention bias toward early tokens in autoregressive TTS decoders, causing the initial audio realization to dominate subsequent generation. To mitigate this effect, we introduce KV-cache swapping and sliding-window attention masking. Experiments demonstrate that our proposed inter-utterance interpolation achieves a 99-100% success rate in gender conversion, up to 36 Hz pitch variation, and up to 1.6 syllables-per-second speed change. Our intra-utterance transition maintains a speaker similarity of 0.81-0.91 and achieves perceptual smoothness scores of 3.48-4.48.

</details>


### [94] [RAG-Coding: Enhancing LLM Medical Coding with Structured External Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27377)
*Yidong Gan,David D. Nguyen,Yang Lin,Peter Zhong,Thanh Vu,Long Duong,Yuan-Fang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RAG-Coding方法，通过协调四个LLM智能体并利用外部编码知识库，在ICD-10-CM自动编码任务上超越现有基线模型8-13%（micro-F1），并发布了更新至2025年指南的MDACE-2025数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ICD-10-CM自动编码方法缺乏对外部编码知识（如官方编码表和指南）的有效利用，导致编码准确性和临床合规性不足。

Method: RAG-Coding协调四个LLM智能体，通过检索和交叉引用官方编码列表及指南等外部知识源来增强编码决策。

Result: 在MDACE数据集上，RAG-Coding在多个LLM骨干上比最佳LLM基线高出8-13%（micro-F1）和2-8%（macro-F1）；相比PLM-ICD，micro召回率高出11%，但micro精确率低6%，整体F1相当。

Conclusion: 融入外部编码知识对提升编码性能至关重要，RAG-Coding方法有效提升了自动编码的准确性和临床合规性。

Abstract: We present RAG-Coding, an agentic method for automated ICD-10-CM coding. RAG-Coding orchestrates four large language model (LLM) agents and grounds their coding decisions in external knowledge sources (e.g. the official coding tabular list and guidelines). By retrieving and cross-referencing relevant knowledge in these sources, the agents enhance coding accuracy and ensure clinical compliance. On the MDACE dataset, RAG-Coding outperforms the best LLM-based baseline by 8-13\% in micro-F1 and 2-8\% in macro-F1 across multiple LLM backbones. Compared to the state-of-the-art pretrained language model method, PLM-ICD, RAG-Coding exhibits higher micro recall (+11\%), while PLM-ICD exhibits higher micro precision (+6\%), yielding comparable micro- and macro-F1. Ablations show stepwise gains, highlighting the importance of incorporating external knowledge. We also release MDACE-2025, updating the original dataset with expert re-annotations with the latest 2025 ICD-10-CM guidelines. This update features more fine-grained code labels and enables evaluation against current clinical standards.

</details>


### [95] [OralAgent: Integrating Reasoning, Tools, and Knowledge for Interactive Dental Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27378)
*Jing Hao,Siyuan Dai,Yongxin Zhang,Yuci Liang,Jiamin Wu,Jiahao Bao,Yuxuan Fan,Zanting Ye,Yanpeng Sun,Xinyu Zhang,Ming Hu,Liang Zhan,James Kit Hon Tsoi,Linlin Shen,Junjun He,Kuo Feng Hung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OralAgent是首个牙科专用AI智能体，集成了22种视觉分析工具和368本经典牙科教科书，实现了多模态推理、工具决策和知识检索的自动化框架；同时构建了OralCorpus文本库和OralQA-ZH中文问答基准，在多个基准测试上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有牙科AI模型针对特定任务和单一成像模态设计，缺乏统一框架，难以在实际临床工作流中落地应用。

Method: 构建OralAgent智能体，集成22个视觉分析工具和368本经典教科书；构建OralCorpus（134.8M tokens的双语牙科文本资源）和OralQA-ZH（798道中文多选题基准）。

Result: 在MMOral-Uni、MMOral-OPG和OralQA-ZH基准上达到最先进水平，展现出色的有效性、可解释性和临床适应性。

Conclusion: OralAgent作为首个牙科专用AI智能体，通过多模态推理、工具调用和知识检索的端到端框架，显著提升了牙科图像分析的自动化水平和临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Dental image analysis plays a pivotal role in supporting accurate diagnosis and treatment planning in oral healthcare. Although recent advances have produced dental AI models for specific tasks and individual imaging modalities, their isolated designs limit practical use in real-world clinical workflows. In this paper, we present OralAgent, the first dental-specialized AI agent that unifies multimodal reasoning, tool-based decision-making, and knowledge-grounded retrieval within an end-to-end automated framework. It integrates 22 visual analysis tools and 368 widely-used classical dental textbooks, enabling autonomous reasoning, planning, tool use, knowledge retrieval, and multi-step workflow execution. Furthermore, we introduce OralCorpus, a large-scale, high-quality bilingual textual resource containing 134.8M tokens curated for dental retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). To evaluate models' multidisciplinary dental knowledge, we construct OralQA-ZH, a Chinese multiple-choice question benchmark consisting of 798 items across eleven oral subspecialties. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OralAgent achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MMOral-Uni, MMOral-OPG, and OralQA-ZH benchmarks, highlighting its effectiveness, interpretability, and adaptability in real-world clinical settings. The code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/isjinghao/OralAgent.

</details>


### [96] [BioELX: Cross-lingual Biomedical Entity Linking via Alias-based Retrieval and LLM Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27380)
*Yi Wang,Corina Dima,Liangyu Zhong,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BioELX是一个两阶段跨语言生物医学实体链接框架，通过多语言别名增强SapBERT训练和LLM重排序，无需标注数据即可实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言生物医学实体链接（BEL）需要大量专家标注数据，成本高昂，尤其对于低资源语言；现有SapBERT检索器主要基于英文别名训练，泛化到非英文提及的能力差，且缺乏上下文感知消歧能力。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage 1 利用Wikidata多语言别名丰富SapBERT训练数据，提升跨语言候选检索；Stage 2 使用预训练LLM作为排序器，联合考虑提及上下文和候选信息进行上下文感知消歧，无需监督训练。

Result: 在五个基准（XL-BEL、EMEA、Patent、WikiMed-DE、MedMentions）上达到SOTA。XL-BEL平均Recall@1提升+19.2，低资源语言提升显著（土耳其语+21.6，韩语+22.1，泰语+30.8），EMEA +6.2，Patent +5.4，WikiMed-DE +12.8。

Conclusion: BioELX有效解决了跨语言BEL中低资源语言泛化差和缺乏上下文消歧的问题，且无需任务特定标注数据。

Abstract: Cross-lingual biomedical entity linking (BEL) maps mentions in any language to unique identifiers in a biomedical knowledge base (KB), supporting clinical and biomedical NLP applications. However, expert-annotated training data for BEL are costly, especially for low-resource languages. Moreover, many cross-lingual BEL systems rely on SapBERT-based retrievers trained on predominantly English aliases in the KB, leading to poor generalization to unseen non-English mentions and limited context-aware disambiguation. We propose BioELX, a two-stage cross-lingual BEL framework that requires no task-specific annotated training corpora. In Stage~1, we enrich SapBERT training with Wikidata-derived multilingual aliases and use the resulting retriever to improve cross-lingual candidate retrieval. In Stage~2, we perform context-aware disambiguation with a pre-trained LLM ranker that jointly considers the mention context and candidate, eliminating the need for supervised training. Experiments on five benchmarks (XL-BEL, EMEA, Patent, WikiMed-DE, and MedMentions) show that BioELX achieves new state-of-the-art performance. It improves average Recall@1 on XL-BEL by +19.2, with especially large gains for low-resource languages, e.g., +21.6 on Turkish, +22.1 on Korean, +30.8 on Thai, and delivers consistent improvements on EMEA (+6.2), Patent (+5.4), and WikiMed-DE (+12.8). Code and resources will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [97] [Bridging the Stability-Expressivity Gap: Synthetic Data Scaling and Preference Alignment for Low-Resource Spoken Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27383)
*Yizhong Geng,Yanliang Li,Jinghan Yang,Tianhan Jiang,Boxun An,Ya Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现口语语言模型在低资源场景下存在"稳定性-表现力鸿沟"：合成数据提升语音准确性的同时会抑制韵律多样性。为此提出两种自对齐框架（DGSA和TDSC）来恢复表现力，成果超越ElevenLabs和Gemini Pro，并首次实现老挝语的零样本语音克隆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口语语言模型在低资源语言中因标注语音稀缺而表现受限，尽管合成数据可提供语音监督，但其对模型表现力的抑制作用尚未被充分认识和研究。

Method: 提出两种自对齐框架：1）解耦引导自对齐（DGSA），通过分离韵律与音色来恢复复杂语言的表现力；2）温度驱动自批判（TDSC），在参考样本极少的场景下通过自动探索与过滤稳定生成。

Result: 所提方法在性能上超越ElevenLabs和Gemini Pro等强商用系统，并首次实现了老挝语的零样本语音克隆。

Conclusion: 合成数据带来的稳定性提升以牺牲表现力为代价，而所提的自对齐方法可有效弥合这一鸿沟，为低资源语言语音合成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Spoken Language Models (SLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for speech synthesis by bypassing explicit grapheme-to-phoneme pipelines. However, their effectiveness in low-resource languages remains fundamentally limited by the scarcity of transcribed speech. In practice, synthetic data has become the primary strategy for scaling SLMs in such settings, providing reliable phonetic supervision when real data is insufficient. In this work, we show that this reliance introduces a fundamental trade-off, which we term the Stability-Expressivity Gap: while synthetic data improves phonetic accuracy, it progressively suppresses prosodic variability, ultimately leading to a collapse of expressivity (Synthetic Erosion). To bridge this gap, we propose two self-alignment frameworks. Disentanglement-Guided Self-Alignment (DGSA) recovers expressivity for complex languages by exploiting prosody-timbre separation. For regimes where authentic references are exceptionally limited, Temperature-Driven Self-Critique (TDSC) stabilizes generation through automated exploration and filtering. Our approach outperforms strong commercial systems, including ElevenLabs and Gemini Pro, and enables the first zero-shot voice cloning capability for Lao.

</details>


### [98] [From AR to Diffusion: Efficiently Adapting Large Language Models with Strictly Causal and Elastic Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27387)
*Xiangyu Ma,Teng Xiao,Zuchao Li,Lefei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FLUID框架通过严格因果对齐技术，使预训练的自回归模型（如GPT）能够高效适配扩散模型范式，实现并行文本生成，大幅降低训练成本并达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能实现高效并行文本生成，但其依赖的双向注意力机制与预训练自回归模型存在结构不匹配，导致无法复用已有AR模型的强大先验知识，需要从头进行昂贵的预训练。

Method: 提出FLUID框架，包含两个关键技术：1) Strictly Causal Alignment（严格因果对齐），实现从标准GPT风格检查点的无缝初始化；2) Elastic Horizons（弹性视野），一种基于信息熵的动态机制，根据局部信息密度自适应调整去噪步长，取代固定调度策略。

Result: 实验表明FLUID达到当前最优性能，同时将训练成本降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: FLUID成功调和了已有AR模型基础与高效并行生成之间的矛盾，为复用预训练AR先验进行扩散模型训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models promise efficient parallel text generation but rely on bidirectional attention, creating a structural mismatch with pre-trained Autoregressive (AR) models. This incompatibility precludes reusing robust AR priors, necessitating prohibitive pre-training from scratch. To bridge this gap, we propose FLUID, a framework that efficiently adapts AR backbones to the diffusion paradigm. By enforcing Strictly Causal Alignment, FLUID enables seamless initialization from standard GPT-style checkpoints, circumventing the need for massive pre-training. Furthermore, we introduce Elastic Horizons, an entropy-driven mechanism that dynamically modulates denoising strides based on local information density rather than fixed schedules. Experiments demonstrate that FLUID achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing training costs by orders of magnitude, effectively reconciling established AR foundations with efficient parallel generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/Oli-lab-nun/FLUID/tree/main.

</details>


### [99] [Modeling Community Attitude through Reaction Tone: A Human-AI Collaborative Framework for Evaluating LLM Alignment with Linguistic Behaviors in Online Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27388)
*Nuan Wen,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE框架，通过评估LLM模拟的社区对话与真实事件反应之间的差距，发现存在持续的“现实主义鸿沟”，即使使用明确的社区提示也无法固有地提高模拟保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将社会身份简化为静态标签，忽视了真实群体如何应对社会变化，缺乏对LLM模拟社区话语保真度的有效评估方法。

Method: 提出CARE（Community-Aware Reaction Evaluation）框架，通过人类-AI协作验证的言外语气和潜在态度频谱，基准测试LLM模拟对话与真实社区对新闻事件反应的差距。

Result: 发现存在持续的“现实主义鸿沟”，使用明确社区提示无法改善模拟保真度；前沿模型间存在不同的行为特征，表明当前对齐策略不足以捕捉在线群体的社会语言学动态。

Conclusion: 现有LLM对齐策略在捕捉在线社区的社会语言学动态方面存在不足，需要新的方法来弥合模拟与真实社区反应之间的差距。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly utilized as proxies for computational social analysis; yet, their ability to faithfully represent the "thick descriptions" (Geertz, 1973) of human communities remains a critical challenge. Current evaluations often reduce social identity to static labels, sidelining how real-world groups navigate social shifts. To bridge this gap, we introduce CARE (Community-Aware Reaction Evaluation), a reaction-centered framework that benchmarks LLM-simulated discourse against the authentic, event-contingent responses of distinct communities to real-world news. By characterizing a fine-grained spectrum of illocutionary tones and the underlying attitudes they manifest--validated through human-AI collaboration--our diagnosis reveals a persistent "realism gap": steering LLMs with explicit community prompts fails to inherently improve simulation fidelity. Analysis further identifies divergent behavioral signatures among frontier models, suggesting that current alignment strategies remain insufficient for capturing the sociolinguistic dynamics of online groups.

</details>


### [100] [EvoSpec: Evolving Speculative Decoding via Real-Time Vocabulary and Parameter AdaptationTarget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27390)
*Shuyu Zhang,Lingfeng Pan,Qicheng Wang,Yaqi Shi,Yueyang Tan,Ruyu Yan,Jiaqi Chen,Lixing Du,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvoSpec通过动态词汇和参数自适应实现了草稿模型的实时进化，在专业领域和话题切换场景下，相比静态剪枝方法显著提升了推测解码的接受率和速度，在EAGLE-3上比先进静态基线FR-Spec快1.13倍，且内存开销降低27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态剪枝方法在专业领域或话题切换场景下，由于无法捕捉动态分布偏移，导致接受率急剧下降，而输出投影层随词汇表规模扩大成为推理瓶颈。

Method: 提出EvoSpec框架，包括：(1) 上下文感知机制，通过高效语义和统计索引检索关键长尾词；(2) 基于课程学习的轻量级在线对齐策略，持续最小化草稿模型与目标模型之间的分布差距。

Result: 在代码、法律、医学等专业领域，EvoSpec在EAGLE-3上比FR-Spec实现1.13倍加速，内存开销比标准在线自适应方法低27%。

Conclusion: EvoSpec克服了静态基线方法的局限性，通过动态自适应有效提升了推测解码在专业领域的性能。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates Large Language Model inference via a draft-then-verify paradigm, yet the output projection layer becomes a bottleneck as vocabulary sizes scale. While existing static pruning methods effectively reduce this overhead, they suffer from precipitous drops in acceptance rate in specialized domains or topic-switching scenarios due to their inability to capture dynamic distribution shifts. To address this, we introduce EvoSpec, a framework that enables real-time evolution of the draft model through dynamic vocabulary and parameter adaptation. Unlike static or purely retrieval-based approaches, EvoSpec employs a context-aware mechanism that retrieves critical long-tail tokens via efficient semantic and statistical indexing. Furthermore, we propose a lightweight online alignment strategy utilizing curriculum learning to continually minimize the distributional gap between the draft and target models. Extensive evaluations across specialized domains (coding, law, and medicine) confirm that EvoSpec overcomes the limitations of static baselines. On EAGLE-3, it achieves a 1.13x speedup in these settings over the state-of-the-art static baseline FR-Spec, with 27\% lower memory overhead than standard online adaptation.

</details>


### [101] [Debate Helps Weak Judges Reward Stronger Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27483)
*Ethan Elasky,Frank Nakasako,Naman Goyal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 辩论作为可扩展监督协议的效果取决于批评者的能力是否超过判断者，以及判断者是否将批评视为需要验证的主张而非需要总结的证词。在满足条件的模型配对中，辩论显著优于咨询基线，而消除反驳回合不会影响性能，表明单一独立批评即可获得大部分收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管辩论在理论上具有可扩展监督的潜力，但实证结果好坏参半，尤其是在判断者没有隐藏信息的情况下效果不明显。本文旨在探究在更强辩论者/更弱判断者设定下，提案者-批评者辩论何时有效以及为何有效。

Method: 在可编程验证的代码和逻辑任务上，采用更强辩论者/更弱判断者的设置，研究提案者-批评者辩论模式。比较辩论与咨询基线，并分析批评者的分类能力、判断者的验证行为对辩论效果的影响。

Result: 当批评者的分类能力超过判断者且判断者将批评视为要验证的主张时，辩论效果显著；否则效果为零。消除反驳回合对性能无显著影响，单一独立批评即可获得大部分收益。

Conclusion: 在可验证领域，存在一种更廉价的训练-free可扩展监督原语（答案、批评、判断），以及一个部署前审计条件（批评者是否击败判断者、判断者是否会验证它）可预测辩论何时会有帮助。

Abstract: Despite theoretical promise, debate as a scalable oversight protocol has produced mixed empirical results: gains in some settings, and null effects in others, especially when the judge does not have information hidden from it. We study proposer-critic debate in a stronger-debater/weaker-judge setting on programmatically verifiable code and logic tasks. Debate helps the judge over a consultancy baseline when the critic provides a usable advantage: the critic's classification ability must exceed the judge's, and the judge must treat critic speeches as claims to verify rather than testimony to summarize.
  On the three of five pairings where the condition holds, proposer-critic debate's gains are statistically significant over consultancy, and these pairings are the most capable model pairings. On the two non-responder pairings in our set, debate produces null effects, and judge verification rates drop by tens of percentage points once a critic enters the transcript. In these cases the critic's binary-classification ability and the judge's are within noise of each other, and the critic's disagreement is parsed as testimony rather than a claim to check.
  Ablating rebuttal rounds from debate produces no measurable change in judge performance: a single independent critique recovers the bulk of debate's benefit at lower inference cost. These findings suggest a cheaper primitive for training-free scalable oversight in verifiable domains (answer, critique, judge) and a pre-deployment audit (does the critic beat the judge, and will the judge verify it?) that predicts when debate will help.

</details>


### [102] [PAST2HARM: A Simple Adaptive Past Tense Attack for Jailbreaking Multimodal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27545)
*Snehasis Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PAST2HARM，一种利用过去时态改写绕过多模态文本到图像模型安全机制的黑盒越狱框架，在Gemini、GPT Image和SD XL上实现了67%-100%的攻击成功率，能生成包括色情、政治虚假信息、仇恨言论等有害内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态AI系统的越狱攻击研究不足，而 unsafe 图像生成相比 unsafe 文本后果更严重，且当前防御机制相对不成熟。

Method: PAST2HARM框架：1）时间深度维度：通过时间深化逐步强化历史锚定和档案线索，侵蚀不同对齐强度模型的拒绝边界；2）迭代升级维度：在获得初始合规后逐步升级，利用语言模型作为裁判测量危害严重性。

Result: Gemini Nano Banana Pro攻击成功率83%，GPT Image 2为67%，SD XL为100%；跨模型迁移成功率超过50%；对话中期转折点形成峰值漏洞窗口。

Conclusion: 当前多模态安全防护存在根本性脆弱性，亟需更强的多模态安全训练。

Abstract: Jailbreak attacks on multimodal AI systems remain underexplored, even though unsafe image generation can have more severe consequences than unsafe text and current defenses are relatively immature. We introduce PAST2HARM, a simple yet effective adaptive jailbreak framework that bypasses refusal training in state of the art multimodal text to image models. Building on prior findings that past tense reformulations can evade safeguards, PAST2HARM systematically exploits this vulnerability in multimodal generative AI.
  We characterize the attack along two dimensions. First, breadth: through temporal deepening, the framework incrementally strengthens historical anchoring and archival cues, eroding refusal boundaries across models with varying alignment strength. Second, depth: via iterative escalation after initial compliance, we probe the upper bound of harmful generation, measuring severity using a scalar severity jailbreak metric evaluated by a language model acting as a judge. We find that mid conversation turns form peak vulnerability windows, where harmfulness increases before plateauing and eventually undergoing semantic inversion.
  We evaluate PAST2HARM on three models Gemini Nano Banana Pro, GPT Image 2, and SD XL achieving attack success rates of 83 percent, 67 percent, and 100 percent in a black box, gradient free setting. Adversarial prompts also transfer across models, with cross model success rates above 50 percent. The attack elicits diverse harmful outputs, including explicit sexual content, political disinformation, historical denial narratives, hate speech, and self harm glorification. We further release a curated benchmark of prompts, reformulations, and outputs as a resource for red teaming and alignment. Our results expose fundamental brittleness in current safeguards and highlight the need for stronger multimodal safety training.

</details>


### [103] [The Future of Facts: Tracing the Factual Generation-Verification Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27564)
*Tim R. Davidson,Anja Surina,Caglar Gulcehre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型在事实知识上的生成-验证差距（GV-gap），发现验证能力始终先于生成能力习得、验证比生成更抗持续学习、事实更新可能导致模型同时验证新旧答案的“多重宇宙”状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注了语言模型的生成-验证差距在自我改进和推理中的作用，但对其在事实知识上的训练动态机制理解不足，作者希望系统探究这一差距的形成机制。

Method: 在四个开源模型族（每种两个规模）上，追踪三个训练阶段（知识获取、持续学习、事实更新）中生成与验证能力的变化，并在前沿模型上进行自然实验验证。

Result: 三个主要发现：验证能力始终先于生成能力习得；验证比生成更鲁棒地抵抗持续学习带来的遗忘；事实更新后模型可能陷入“多重宇宙”状态，同时验证新旧答案正确。前沿模型实验还发现对常见事实存在残留验证偏差。

Conclusion: 验证与生成能力在事实知识上存在系统性的训练不对称性，这对语言模型的知识更新、自我改进和可靠性设计有重要启示。

Abstract: Language models are becoming the default interface to factual knowledge, yet they often verify outputs more reliably than they generate them. This generation-verification gap (GV-gap) underlies many recent advances in self-improvement and reasoning, but its dynamics on factual knowledge specifically remain poorly understood. We focus on the training mechanisms underlying factual GV-gaps, distinguishing them from their computational and aesthetic counterparts. We trace generation and verification capabilities through three training phases (acquisition, continual learning, and updating) across four open-source model families at two scales each. Three findings recur across models: (i) verification is consistently learned before generation; (ii) verification is more robust to continual learning than generation; and (iii) factual updates can leave models in a "multi-verse" state, simultaneously verifying both old and new answers as correct. Natural experiments on frontier models reproduce these dynamics at scale and reveal residual verification biases on well-covered facts.

</details>


### [104] [Can Hallucinations Be Useful? Solving Multi-Hop Questions With SLMs By Chaining System-I/II Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27596)
*Saptarshi Sengupta,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 小语言模型（SLM）采用"先回答后推理"的策略，通过System-I快速给出初始答案，再利用System-II基于检索证据进行深度推理，相比传统的"先思考后检索"方法，在复杂多步推理任务上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对SLM在复杂多步推理中容易因早期幻觉导致错误级联的问题，挑战"先思考后检索"的主流策略，探索更高效的推理范式。

Method: 提出一种认知启发的双系统框架：System-I（零样本）快速生成初始答案，System-II基于该假设从知识源检索证据并进行深度推理，从而修正或确认最终答案。

Result: 在多个多步问答基准测试上，该方法优于传统的"先思考后检索"的现有工作。

Conclusion: SLM在初始答案上常常具有准确的置信度，且幻觉有时反而有助于逼近正确答案，因此"先回答后推理"是一种更有效的策略。

Abstract: Recently, there has been increased interest in Small Language Models (SLMs), which are fast, show good performance, and have lower hardware demands than large language models (LLMs). However, SLMs hallucinate more frequently than LLMs, impacting their ability to solve complex multi-step reasoning problems as early mistakes cascade to the final response. To address this, existing works think-first followed by iterative retrieval to reduce hallucination. We argue that the think-first strategy is not always necessary as we find that: (i) SLMs are often accurately confident in their initial answer and, (ii) hallucinations can actually be beneficial for honing in on the true answer. As such, we position our work as an inversion of this strategy, i.e., answer first-reason later. We propose a cognitively-inspired framework where the model is first allowed to quickly answer the question (System-I (zero-shot)) and then resorts to deeper thinking (System-II) based on evidence retrieved from a knowledge source using the initial hypothesis. By combining System-I and System-II style thinking, we show that our method can outperform prior work that takes the traditional think-first route on various multi-step question-answering benchmarks.

</details>


### [105] [Simorgh at SemEval-2026 task 7: Region-Aware Hybrid Retrieval for Low-Resource Cultural Reasoning in Multilingual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27636)
*Hadi Bayrami Asl Tekanlou,Mahdi Bakhtiyarzadeh,Jafar Razmara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言文化常识问答中大型语言模型的局限性，提出了一种结合BM25、稠密语义匹配和区域加权启发式的混合检索方法，并采用Qwen3-14B量化模型进行结构化提示回答。实验表明混合检索提升了跨语言稳定性，但数据不平衡问题仍未完全解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在通用推理任务中表现优异，但在处理低资源语言中具有文化根基的知识时面临挑战，现有方法对跨语言文化常识问答的适配性不足。

Method: 使用BLEnD多语言基准（30种语言、多社会文化领域），提出区域感知混合检索方法（BM25+稠密语义匹配+区域加权启发式），检索结果构建结构化提示输入Qwen3-14B量化模型，采用基于logit的确定性答案选择。

Result: 混合检索方法相比纯参数推理提升了跨语言稳定性，但不同语言之间的性能差距依然显著，低资源语言表现较差。

Conclusion: 检索增强方法在缓解训练数据不平衡问题上仍有局限，跨语言文化常识问答的性能差距尚未被完全克服。

Abstract: Although Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate excellent capabilities and performance for general reasoning tasks within the general public domain, they may face challenges with culturally grounded knowledge within languages with limited digital and textual data. In this paper, we investigate culturally grounded multiple-choice question answering with the BLEnD benchmark, which consists of a multilingual corpus of 30 languages and covers various socio-cultural domains, such as cuisine, sports, family, etc. We propose a region-aware hybrid retrieval approach that combines BM25 lexical matching and dense semantic similarity with regional weighting heuristics to improve the relevance of the answer. The retrieved documents are used to construct a structured prompt for the Qwen3-14B quantized model with logit-based deterministic answer selection. The experimental results show improvements to cross-lingual stability with the hybrid retrieval approach over pure parametric inference for culturally grounded question answering. However, there are still notable performance gaps between languages with more and less training data. This shows that the limitations of the retrieval augmentation approach are not entirely overcome by the training data imbalance problem.

</details>


### [106] [Learning to Translate from Soft to Hard LLM Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27642)
*Pitipat Kongsomjit,Suryansh Goyal,Jacob Whitehill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将软提示训练为自然语言翻译模型的方法，不仅能提高软提示的可解释性，还能将小模型上优化的软提示转化为文本提示，部署在大模型上时表现优于原软提示甚至少样本学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软提示调优虽然高效但缺乏可解释性，现有解释方法翻译质量有限，需要更好的翻译模型来提升软提示的可解释性和实用性。

Method: 训练一个专门的软提示到自然语言的翻译模型，在多个数据集（DoDs）上进行定性和定量比较。

Result: 翻译模型生成的言语化结果在流畅性和准确性上优于InSPEcT等无训练方法；小模型优化的软提示翻译后部署到大模型上，性能超过原软提示甚至少样本学习。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅推进了软提示的可解释性，还展示了将软提示跨模型迁移的实际应用价值，为参数高效微调与提示工程的结合提供了新思路。

Abstract: Soft prompt tuning is a parameter-efficient method for adapting LLMs to specific tasks, but suffers from a lack of interpretability. Building on recent work on interpreting soft prompts (Ramati et al., 2024), we explore how training a dedicated soft prompt to natural language translation model can yield higher translation quality. In particular, in both quantitative and qualitative comparisons on multiple Datasets of Datasets (DoDs), we demonstrate that our translator produces fluent, accurate verbalizations that outperforms existing training-free methods like InSPEcT. In addition to advancing interpretability, our work suggests a promising downstream application: soft prompts optimized on small, open-source models can be translated into portable text prompts that, when deployed on larger closed-API models, exceed the performance of the original soft prompt and, in some cases, even few-shot learning.

</details>


### [107] [Disentangling Language Roles in Multilingual LLM Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27649)
*Qishi Zhan,Minxuan Hu,Seoyeon Jang,Lei Zhao,Ziheng Chen,Man Liang,Xinyue Xiang,Jiaxin Liu,Guansu Wang,Liang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了MTM-Bench基准测试，通过完全交叉的语言三元组设计（指令、内容、响应语言），系统评估多语言LLM在不同语言角色组合下的任务执行能力，发现响应语言角色是性能下降的主导因素，且不匹配数量并非单调预测难度的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试未能系统隔离指令、源内容与响应语言三种角色在任务执行中的独立影响，缺乏完全交叉设计的评估框架来揭示语言角色对多语言LLM性能的具体作用机制。

Method: 构建MTM-Bench基准，采用完全交叉的三元组设计（指令语言、内容语言、响应语言），覆盖英语、西班牙语和中文共27种组合，包含2430个测试实例，评估20个前沿和开源LLM，使用语义正确性、目标语言遵循度、约束满足度等多维度分解指标，并通过人工审计验证评分。

Result: 响应语言角色是性能变异的主导轴，单个响应槽不匹配即可造成大部分性能下降；不匹配数量并非单调预测任务难度的指标，模型层面的排序因系统而异；不同任务族通过不同渠道失败，语义正确性不能单独反映可靠的多语言任务执行能力。

Conclusion: 多语言LLM的性能退化主要由语言在任务结构中的角色而非不匹配数量决定，响应语言角色最为关键，评估多语言任务执行需要超越语义正确性，采纳多维度指标。

Abstract: Multilingual LLMs are increasingly used when instruction, source content, and required response languages do not coincide. Existing benchmarks have expanded multilingual instruction-following evaluation, but they rarely isolate these three roles within a fully crossed design. We introduce MTM-Bench, a controlled benchmark for language-conditioned task execution in which each instance is defined by a triplet \((L_{\text{instr}}, L_{\text{content}}, L_{\text{resp}})\). Across English, Spanish, and Chinese, MTM-Bench enumerates all 27 triplets and contains 2{,}430 instances per model across semantic reversal, final-state extraction, and language purity with update realization. We evaluate 20 frontier and open-weight LLMs using decomposed metrics for semantic correctness, target-language adherence, constraint satisfaction, contamination ratio, and joint success, with scoring validated by a targeted human audit. The fully crossed design reveals that degradation is organized by the role a language occupies in the task structure, not merely by mismatch count. The response-language role is the dominant axis of variation, and a single response-slot mismatch accounts for most degradation. The response-only and full-mismatch comparison suggests that mismatch count is not a monotonic predictor of difficulty, with model-level ordering varying across systems. Task families fail through distinct channels, showing that semantic correctness alone does not capture reliable multilingual task execution.

</details>


### [108] [Cultural Fidelity in English-to-Hindi Translation: A Preservation-Fluency Frontier for Gender Recoverability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27654)
*Samyak Savi,Chavi Gupta,Shreyas Gantayet,Tanay Sodha,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式翻译系统在英语到印地语翻译中如何保留源语言的性别信息，提出了两种推理时干预方法（SAR和PAR），在性别保留率上从约10-16%提升至49-81%，但以牺牲流畅性为代价，揭示了文化翻译中保真度与流畅性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式翻译系统作为文化技术，在处理社会性意义的语言线索（如性别信息）时表现不理想。作者希望检验系统能否在英语到印地语翻译中保留源语言明确编码的性别信息，并提出改进方法。

Method: 构建了一个包含37,345个实例、涵盖12个类别的基准数据集；设计了两种机制感知的推理时干预方法：源语言感知重排序器（SAR）和现象感知重排序器（PAR），分别在GPT-4o-mini和Sarvam两个系统上进行测试，并进行人工评估。

Result: PAR在目标子集上性别保留率从11.07%提升至54.47%（GPT-4o-mini），从15.99%提升至49.66%（Sarvam）；人工评估显示性别保留率从10.3%提升至81.3%，但平均流畅度从4.36降至3.37。

Conclusion: 两种干预方法位于保留率与流畅性之间的前沿曲线上，而非存在单一最优方案。文化嵌入的生成任务需要在保真度、流畅性和风格自然性之间做出明确权衡。

Abstract: Generative translation systems are cultural technologies because they decide how socially meaningful cues are rendered within culturally specific grammatical systems. We study one concrete notion of successful cultural translation: when an English source explicitly encodes gender, an English-to-Hindi translation should preserve the recoverability of that cue unless the source itself is ambiguous. We evaluate this criterion on a 37,345-instance benchmark spanning twelve categories and show that five systems frequently erase gender through ergative and honorific constructions. We then introduce two mechanism-aware inference-time interventions. The first, the Source-Aware Reranker (SAR), prefers candidates that avoid gender-neutralizing syntax. The second, the Phenomenon-Aware Reranker (PAR), preserves gender through targeted lexical marking even when ergative syntax remains. Across GPT-4o-mini and Sarvam, PAR improves target-subset accuracy from 11.07% to 54.47% and from 15.99% to 49.66%, respectively. Human evaluation shows that PAR increases gender preservation from 10.3% to 81.3%, but reduces mean fluency from 4.36 to 3.37. These findings place the two interventions on a preservation and fluency frontier rather than supporting a single dominant solution, and show how culturally situated generation can require explicit tradeoffs among fidelity, fluency, and stylistic naturalness.

</details>


### [109] [TRACES: Proactive Safety Auditing for Multi-Turn LLM Agents via Trajectory-State Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27690)
*Jiaqian Li,Yanshu Li,Boxuan Zhang,Ruixiang Tang,Kuan-Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TRACES，一种基于表示学习的前瞻性审计器，通过观察LLM的隐藏表示学习前缀级轨迹风险状态，在弱监督下实现密集风险估计，提升智能体安全性预测与主动风险判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在多步工具使用和环境交互中，安全风险常在中间步骤悄然出现，事后审计（post-hoc diagnosis）往往错过风险正在发生时的预警时机，因此需要前瞻性的主动审计方法。

Method: TRACES利用观察LLM的隐藏表示，从中归纳潜在机制特征（latent mechanism features），建模其时间演化以判断部分轨迹是否正在偏移至不安全行为。训练时仅需弱轨迹级监督，即可生成密集的前缀级风险估计。

Result: 在多个智能体安全基准上，TRACES在全轨迹安全预测和主动风险判别方面均取得提升。进一步分析表明，学习到的风险状态可用于训练更安全的智能体。

Conclusion: 主动审计对长程智能体安全具有重要潜力，TRACES为前瞻性风险监测提供了一种有效且低标注成本的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate through multi-turn tool use and environment interaction, where safety risks often emerge from intermediate steps long before they surface in the final outcome. Reactive auditing is therefore insufficient: post-hoc diagnosis frequently misses the chance to flag risks while they are unfolding. We propose TRACES, a representation-based proactive auditor that learns prefix-level trajectory risk states from the hidden representations of an observer LLM. TRACES induces latent mechanism features from step representations and models their temporal evolution to estimate whether a partial trajectory is drifting toward unsafe behavior. To sidestep the cost and ambiguity of step-level risk annotation, TRACES is trained with weak trajectory-level supervision while still producing dense prefix-level risk estimates. Across multiple agent safety benchmarks, TRACES improves both full-trajectory safety prediction and proactive risk discrimination. Our analyses further suggest that these risk states can help train a safer agent, highlighting the broader potential of proactive auditing for long-horizon agent safety.

</details>


### [110] [Chain-based Adaptive Reconfiguration Over Lattices for Hallucination Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27706)
*Joan Vendrell Gallart,Solmaz Kia,Russell Bent,Michael Grosskopf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CAROL框架，通过在语义格上构建字符串子模目标，将幻觉缓解转化为马尔可夫链接受-拒绝过程，在测试时有效减少大语言模型幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉缓解方法多依赖token级不确定性或检索增强，缺乏语义层面的统一检测与缓解框架，且缺乏理论保证。

Method: 基于语义一致性定义新的不确定性度量，构建文本序列格上的字符串子模目标，通过马尔可夫链接受-拒绝过程迭代优化输出。

Result: 在QA和多智能体推理基准上，相比基于似然和检索增强的基线方法，显著减少幻觉，提升可靠性与可解释性，同时保持计算效率。

Conclusion: CAROL将幻觉检测与缓解统一在语义层面，具有可证明的收敛性和近最优性保证，是一种高效且可靠的测试时幻觉减少框架。

Abstract: We introduce CAROL (Chain-based Adaptive Reconfiguration Over Lattices), a probabilistic framework for test-time hallucination reduction in large language models. Rather than relying on token-level uncertainty, CAROL defines a semantic uncertainty measure based on the consistency between generated responses and a trusted context, inducing a string-submodular objective over a lattice of textual sequences. This formulation enables hallucination mitigation to be cast as a Markov chain accept-reject process with provable convergence and near-optimality guarantees, allowing the model to iteratively refine outputs toward semantic consistency. By operating at the level of meaning, CAROL unifies hallucination detection and mitigation within a single framework. Empirical results on question answering and multi-agent reasoning benchmarks show that CAROL significantly reduces hallucinations and improves reliability and interpretability compared to likelihood-based and retrieval-augmented baselines, while maintaining competitive computational efficiency.

</details>


### [111] [ReverseMath: Answer Inversion for Scalable and Verifiable Mathematical Problem Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27709)
*Raoyuan Zhao,Yihong Liu,Yupei Du,Hinrich Schütze,Michael A. Hedderich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ReverseMath方法，通过反转原问题中的答案与条件关系，自动生成带正确标注的新数学问题，既可用于评估模型是否依赖记忆，也可作为强化学习的可验证训练数据，提升数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准多为静态数据，模型可能通过记忆而非真正推理作答；手动构造新问题成本高，需要可扩展的自动化问题生成方法。

Method: 通过答案反演（Answer Inversion）：将原问题中的某个数值屏蔽，把原答案作为已知条件，重写问题使屏蔽值成为新答案，从而生成输入-输出关系反转的新问题。

Result: 评估方面，原始问题与反转问题配对比对发现模型行为存在显著差异，甚至错误输出原答案，暗示记忆行为；训练方面，将ReverseMath生成的数据作为RL数据增强，在多个基准上提升了数学推理性能。

Conclusion: ReverseMath是一种有效的分析工具和可扩展的可验证训练数据来源，有助于区分记忆与推理，并提升LLM的数学推理能力。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning benchmarks are vital for evaluating large language models (LLMs), but many are static and repeatedly exposed through public evaluation and training pipelines, making it difficult to separate genuine reasoning from memorization. Meanwhile, manually constructing new math problems with reliable answers remains costly. We introduce ReverseMath, a scalable method for generating new math problems through answer inversion. Given a problem and its answer, ReverseMath masks a numerical value in the original problem, treats the original answer as a known condition, and rewrites the problem so that the masked value becomes the new answer. The generated problem reverses the original input-output relation, making its answer known by construction. We study ReverseMath for both evaluation and training. For evaluation, paired original/reversed problems reveal substantial behavioral shifts: models sometimes fail on reversed problems and even incorrectly output the original answer, suggesting memorization-like behavior. For training, ReverseMath provides automatically labeled reversed problems as data augmentation for reinforcement learning (RL). Experiments show that including ReverseMath-generated data improves mathematical reasoning performance across multiple benchmarks, demonstrating its value as both an analysis tool and a scalable source of verifiable training data.

</details>


### [112] [Beyond Input Understanding: Diagnosing Multilingual Mathematical Reasoning with Directed Acyclic Trace Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27715)
*Jiaqiao Zhang,Zhoujun Li,Raoyuan Zhao,Jian Lan,Thomas Seidl,Michael A. Hedderich,Hinrich Schütze,Yihong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大型推理模型在非英语语言中数学推理表现差，不仅是语言理解问题，更是推理执行本身受影响。为此提出DATG框架分析推理依赖关系，并设计两种测试时控制方法提升低资源语言推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为LRMs在非英语语言上数学推理表现差是因为语言理解失败，但作者发现即使在英语题目下，控制推理语言也会影响准确率，说明语言还影响推理执行本身，需要系统研究这一现象。

Method: 提出DATG（有向无环跟踪图）框架，将推理轨迹映射为语言无关的数学锚点和依赖关系，通过比对目标语言轨迹与参考DAG，度量锚点覆盖、依赖边遵循和有害数学动作等指标。并设计Loop-Retry和Formula-Retry两种测试时控制方法。

Result: 在Qwen3系列模型上对12种语言进行实验，发现非英语推理存在锚点覆盖降低和依赖保真度减弱的问题，尤其在低资源语言中更明显。Loop-Retry和Formula-Retry能持续提升低资源语言的推理性能。

Conclusion: 语言不仅影响数学问题的理解，也影响推理执行过程。DATG框架能有效诊断不同语言下的推理缺陷，针对性的测试时控制方法可缓解低资源语言上的性能下降。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) achieve strong mathematical reasoning performance in English, but remain much less reliable in many low- and medium-resource languages. This gap is often explained as a failure to understand non-English problem statements. We show that this view is incomplete: even when the problem is given in English, controlling the model's reasoning language can substantially reduce accuracy, suggesting that language also affects reasoning execution itself.
